Method code:
457 |     @Override
458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
460 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
462 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
463 | 
464 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
465 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
466 | 
467 |         L in = predNodeState.out.get(
468 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
469 | 
470 |         values.add(in);
471 |       }
472 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }
477 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1886-1888
Documentation:
/**
   * Is this node a function declaration? A function declaration is a function
   * that has a name that is added to the current scope (i.e. a function that
   * is not part of a expression; see {@link #isFunctionExpression}).
   */
Method code:
1886 |   static boolean isFunctionDeclaration(Node n) {
1887 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && isStatement(n);
1888 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 166-206
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
166 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
167 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
168 |     switch (n.getType()) {
169 |       case Token.STRING:
170 |         return n.getString();
171 | 
172 |       case Token.NAME:
173 |         String name = n.getString();
174 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
175 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
176 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
177 |           return name;
178 |         }
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.NUMBER:
182 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
183 | 
184 |       case Token.FALSE:
185 |       case Token.TRUE:
186 |       case Token.NULL:
187 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
188 | 
189 |       case Token.VOID:
190 |         return "undefined";
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NOT:
193 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
194 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
195 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
196 |         }
197 |         break;
198 | 
199 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
200 |         return arrayToString(n);
201 | 
202 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
203 |         return "[object Object]";
204 |     }
205 |     return null;
206 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 119-155
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
119 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
120 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
121 |                 Scope functionScope,
122 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
123 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
124 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
125 |     this.compiler = compiler;
126 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
127 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
128 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
129 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
130 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
131 | 
132 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
133 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
134 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
135 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
140 |     // type is VOID.
141 |     Iterator<Var> varIt =
142 |         functionScope.getDeclarativelyUnboundVarsWithoutTypes();
143 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
144 |       Var var = varIt.next();
145 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
146 |         continue;
147 |       }
148 | 
149 |       this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
150 |           var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
151 |     }
152 | 
153 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
154 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
155 |   }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 423-449
Documentation:

Method code:
423 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
424 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
425 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
426 |     switch (n.getType()) {
427 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
428 |         // normal type checking
429 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
432 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
433 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
434 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
435 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
436 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
437 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
438 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
439 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
440 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
441 |         }
442 | 
443 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
444 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
445 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
446 |         break;
447 |     }
448 |     return true;
449 |   }

   * Is this node a hoisted function declaration? A function declaration in the

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 842-947
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
842 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
843 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
844 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
845 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
846 | 
847 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
848 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
849 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
850 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
851 | 
852 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
853 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
854 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
855 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
856 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
857 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
858 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
859 |         }
860 |       }
861 | 
862 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
863 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
864 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
865 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
866 |             property, rvalue);
867 |         return;
868 |       }
869 | 
870 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
871 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
872 |         checkEnumInitializer(
873 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
874 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
875 |         return;
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       // object.prototype = ...;
879 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
880 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
881 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
882 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
883 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
884 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
885 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
886 |           }
887 |         } else {
888 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
889 |         }
890 |         return;
891 |       }
892 | 
893 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
894 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
895 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
896 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
897 | 
898 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
899 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
900 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
901 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
902 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
903 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
904 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
905 |             }
906 |           } else {
907 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
908 |           }
909 |           return;
910 |         }
911 |       }
912 | 
913 |       // object.property = ...;
914 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
915 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
916 |       if (type != null) {
917 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
918 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
919 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
920 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
921 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
922 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
923 |         }
924 |         return;
925 |       }
926 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
927 |       // variable with inferred type case
928 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
929 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
930 |       if (var != null) {
931 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
932 |           return;
933 |         }
934 |       }
935 |     }
936 | 
937 |     // fall through case
938 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
939 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
940 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
941 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
942 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
943 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
944 |     } else {
945 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
946 |     }
947 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)

Method code:
652 |     @Override
653 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
654 |       Node n =  newNode(
655 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
656 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
657 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
658 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
659 |       // doesn't do.
660 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
661 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
662 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
663 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
664 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
665 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
666 |       return n;
667 |     }

Method code:
296 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
297 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
298 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
299 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
300 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
301 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
302 |     } else {
303 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
304 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
305 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
306 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
307 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
308 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
309 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
310 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
311 |       }
312 |     }
313 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
241 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
242 |       options.checkTypes = true;
243 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = false;
245 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
246 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
247 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
248 |       // parser type warnings.
249 |       options.setWarningLevel(
250 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
251 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
252 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
256 |       options.setWarningLevel(
257 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
258 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
262 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
263 |     guards.add(
264 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
265 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
266 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
267 | 
268 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
269 | 
270 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
271 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
272 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
273 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
274 |     // error off.
275 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
276 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
277 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
282 |   }

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1882-1906
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1882 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1883 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1884 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1885 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1886 |             type.isUnknownType());
1887 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1888 |     if (info != null) {
1889 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1890 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1891 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1892 |         type = infoType;
1893 |       }
1894 | 
1895 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1896 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1897 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1898 |         compiler.report(
1899 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1900 |       }
1901 |     }
1902 | 
1903 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1904 |       n.setJSType(type);
1905 |     }
1906 |   }

/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1718 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1719 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1720 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1721 |     }
1722 | 
1723 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1724 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1725 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1726 |       skipEOLs();
1727 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1728 |         next();
1729 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1730 |       }
1731 |     }
1732 | 
1733 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1734 | 
1735 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1736 |       next();
1737 |       skipEOLs();
1738 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1739 |       if (memberType != null) {
1740 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1741 | 
1742 |         skipEOLs();
1743 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1744 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1745 |         }
1746 | 
1747 |         next();
1748 |       }
1749 |     }
1750 |     return typeNameNode;
1751 |   }

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

176 |   FlowScope flowThrough(Node n, FlowScope input) {

178 |     // want to infer anything about this scope.
179 |     if (input == bottomScope) {
180 |       return input;

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1685-1711
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1685 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1686 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1687 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1688 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1689 |       skipEOLs();
1690 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1691 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1692 |       skipEOLs();
1693 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1694 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1695 |       skipEOLs();
1696 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1697 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1698 |       String string = stream.getString();
1699 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1700 |         skipEOLs();
1701 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1702 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1703 |         return newStringNode(string);
1704 |       } else {
1705 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1706 |       }
1707 |     }
1708 | 
1709 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1710 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1711 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 738-775
Documentation:

Method code:
738 |     @Override
739 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
740 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
741 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
742 |       }
743 | 
744 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
745 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
746 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
747 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
748 |             reportGetter(el);
749 |             continue;
750 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
751 |             reportSetter(el);
752 |             continue;
753 |           }
754 |         }
755 | 
756 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
757 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
758 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
759 |           key.setType(Token.GET);
760 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
761 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
762 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
763 |           }
764 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
765 |           key.setType(Token.SET);
766 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
767 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
768 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
769 |           }
770 |         }
771 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
772 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
773 |       }
774 |       return node;
775 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.defineInferredProperty(String,JSType,boolean,Node)

Original range: 272-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs
   *        property could be called, so setting this incorrectly could result
   *        in live code being removed.
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the inferred definition of
   *        property that might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
272 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,
273 |       JSType type, boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {
274 |     if (hasProperty(propertyName)) {
275 |       JSType originalType = getPropertyType(propertyName);
276 |       type = originalType == null ? type :
277 |           originalType.getLeastSupertype(type);
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, true, inExterns,
281 |         propertyNode);
282 | 
283 |     // All property definitions go through this method
284 |     // or defineDeclaredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
285 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
286 |     // to register this after defining the property.
287 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
288 | 
289 |     return result;
290 |   }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1591-1607
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1591 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1592 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1593 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1594 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1595 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1596 |         next();
1597 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1598 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1599 |           next();
1600 |         }
1601 |         skipEOLs();
1602 |         token = next();
1603 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1604 |       }
1605 |     }
1606 |     return typeExpr;
1607 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JoinOp.BinaryJoinOp.apply(List<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JoinOp.java
Original range: 35-49
Documentation:

Method code:
35 |     @Override
36 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
37 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
38 |       int size = values.size();
39 |       if (size == 1) {
40 |         return values.get(0);
41 |       } else if (size == 2) {
42 |         return apply(values.get(0), values.get(1));
43 |       } else {
44 |         int mid = computeMidPoint(size);
45 |         return apply(
46 |             apply(values.subList(0, mid)),
47 |             apply(values.subList(mid, size)));
48 |       }
49 |     }

/**
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
222 |     private void normalizeObjectLiteralKeyAnnotations(

226 |           value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
227 |         value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
228 |       }
229 |     }

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 900-921
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
901 |     switch (n.getType()) {
902 |       case Token.CALL:
903 |       case Token.GETPROP:
904 |       case Token.GETELEM:
905 |       case Token.THROW:
906 |       case Token.NEW:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
908 |       case Token.INC:
909 |       case Token.DEC:
910 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
911 |         return true;
912 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
913 |         return false;
914 |     }
915 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
916 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
917 |         return true;
918 |       }
919 |     }
920 |     return false;
921 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.

===== M0169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

819 |   private void doPercentTypedAccounting(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
820 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();

831 |     }

===== M0192 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 680-722
Documentation:

Method code:
680 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
681 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
682 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
683 | 
684 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null; name = name.getNext()) {
685 |       scope = traverse(name.getFirstChild(), scope);
686 |     }
687 | 
688 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
689 |     // type casts.
690 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
691 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
692 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
693 |     // we can check for here.
694 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
695 |     if (objectType == null) {
696 |       return scope;
697 |     }
698 | 
699 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
700 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
701 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
702 |       return scope;
703 |     }
704 | 
705 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
706 |          name = name.getNext()) {
707 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
708 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(name);
709 |       if (memberName != null) {
710 |         JSType rawValueType =  name.getFirstChild().getJSType();
711 |         JSType valueType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
712 |             name, rawValueType);
713 |         if (valueType == null) {
714 |           valueType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
715 |         }
716 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, valueType, false, name);
717 |       } else {
718 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
719 |       }
720 |     }
721 |     return scope;
722 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1220-1249
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1220 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1221 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1222 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1223 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1224 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1225 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1226 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1227 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1228 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1229 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1230 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1231 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1232 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1233 |       return false;
1234 |     }
1235 | 
1236 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1237 |     if (type == null) {
1238 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1239 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1240 |       if (var != null) {
1241 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1242 |         if (varType != null) {
1243 |           type = varType;
1244 |         }
1245 |       }
1246 |     }
1247 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1248 |     return true;
1249 |   }

518 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
519 |       switch (n.getType()) {
520 |         case Token.NULL:
521 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
522 |           break;
523 |

545 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2096-2122
Documentation:
/**
   * @param key A OBJECTLIT key node.
   * @return The type expected when using the key.
   */
Method code:
2096 |   static JSType getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node key, JSType valueType) {
2097 |     if (valueType != null) {
2098 |       switch (key.getType()) {
2099 |         case Token.GET:
2100 |           // GET must always return a function type.
2101 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2102 |             FunctionType fntype = ((FunctionType) valueType);
2103 |             valueType = fntype.getReturnType();
2104 |           } else {
2105 |             return null;
2106 |           }
2107 |           break;
2108 |         case Token.SET:
2109 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2110 |             // SET must always return a function type.
2111 |             FunctionType fntype = ((FunctionType) valueType);
2112 |             Node param = fntype.getParametersNode().getFirstChild();
2113 |             // SET function must always have one parameter.
2114 |             valueType = param.getJSType();
2115 |           } else {
2116 |             return null;
2117 |           }
2118 |           break;
2119 |       }
2120 |     }
2121 |     return valueType;
2122 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 978-1023
Documentation:

Method code:
978 |   @Override
979 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
980 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this);
981 | 
982 |     call = (ArrowType) safeResolve(call, t, scope);
983 |     prototype = (FunctionPrototypeType) safeResolve(prototype, t, scope);
984 | 
985 |     // Warning about typeOfThis if it doesn't resolve to an ObjectType
986 |     // is handled further upstream.
987 |     //
988 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
989 |     //
990 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
991 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
992 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
993 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
994 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
995 |     // the name for them.
996 |     JSType maybeTypeOfThis = safeResolve(typeOfThis, t, scope);
997 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
998 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
999 |     }
1000 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1001 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
1002 |     }
1003 | 
1004 |     boolean changed = false;
1005 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1006 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1007 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1008 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1009 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1010 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1011 |     }
1012 |     if (changed) {
1013 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1017 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1018 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));
1019 |       }
1020 |     }
1021 | 
1022 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1023 |   }

===== M0283 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 958-1005
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an object literal field definition <code>key : value</code>.
   *
   * If the <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the
   * schema of the object type it is referring to.
   *
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param key the assign node
   */
Method code:
958 |   private void visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal t, Node key, Node objlit) {
959 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Validate get and set function declarations are valid
960 |     // as is the functions can have "extraneous" bits.
961 | 
962 |     // For getter and setter property definitions the
963 |     // rvalue type != the property type.
964 |     Node rvalue = key.getFirstChild();
965 |     JSType rightType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
966 |         key, getJSType(rvalue));
967 |     if (rightType == null) {
968 |       rightType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
969 |     }
970 | 
971 |     Node owner = objlit;
972 | 
973 |     // Validate value is assignable to the key type.
974 | 
975 |     JSType keyType = getJSType(key);
976 |     boolean valid = validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(t, key,
977 |         rightType, keyType,
978 |         owner, NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key));
979 |     if (valid) {
980 |       ensureTyped(t, key, rightType);
981 |     } else {
982 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
983 |     }
984 | 
985 |     // Validate that the key type is assignable to the object property type.
986 |     // This is necessary as the objlit may have been cast to a non-literal
987 |     // object type.
988 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider introducing a CAST node to the AST (or
989 |     // perhaps a parentheses node).
990 | 
991 |     JSType objlitType = getJSType(objlit);
992 |     ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
993 |         objlitType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
994 |     if (type != null) {
995 |       String property = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key);
996 |       if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
997 |           !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
998 |           !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
999 |         validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
1000 |             t, key, keyType,
1001 |             type.getPropertyType(property), owner, property);
1002 |       }
1003 |       return;
1004 |     }
1005 |   }

/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
868 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
869 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
870 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
874 | 
875 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
876 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
877 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
878 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
879 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
880 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);

882 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
883 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
884 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
885 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
886 |         } else {

===== M0288 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

86 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
87 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
88 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
89 |       addGuards(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards.descendingSet());
90 |     } else {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java

/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
2180 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2181 |     do {
2182 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
2183 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
2184 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
2185 |         return token;
2186 |       }
2187 |       token = next();
2188 |     } while (true);
2189 |   }

510 |   @Override
511 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
512 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
513 | 
514 |     boolean changed = false;
515 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
516 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {
517 |       JSType newAlternate = alternate.resolve(t, scope);
518 |       changed |= (alternate != newAlternate);
519 |       resolvedTypes.add(alternate);
520 |     }
521 |     if (changed) {
522 |       Collection<JSType> newAlternates = resolvedTypes.build();
523 |       Preconditions.checkState(
524 |           newAlternates.hashCode() == this.hashcode);
525 |       alternates = newAlternates;
526 |     }
527 |     return this;
528 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 621-640
Documentation:

Method code:
621 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
622 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
623 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
624 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
625 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |       if (typeSet == null) {
627 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
628 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |       }
630 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
631 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
632 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
633 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
634 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
635 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
636 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
637 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
638 |       }
639 |     }
640 |   }

392 |     @Override
393 |     protected void initialize() {
394 |       orderedWorkSet.clear();

396 |         int outEdgeCount = getCfg().getOutEdges(node.getValue()).size();

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)

Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 188-300
Documentation:

Method code:
188 |   @Override
189 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
190 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
191 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
192 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
193 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
194 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
195 |     // worthwhile.
196 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
197 |     Node condition = null;
198 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
199 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
200 | 
201 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
202 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
203 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
204 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
205 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
206 | 
207 |       switch (branch) {
208 |         case ON_TRUE:
209 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
210 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
211 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
212 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
213 | 
214 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
215 | 
216 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
217 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
218 |             }
219 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
220 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
221 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
222 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
223 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
224 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
225 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
226 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
227 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
228 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
229 |                 }
230 |               }
231 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
232 |             }
233 |             newScope = informed;
234 |             break;
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           // FALL THROUGH
238 | 
239 |         case ON_FALSE:
240 |           if (condition == null) {
241 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
242 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
243 |               condition = source;
244 | 
245 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
246 |               // of the loop.
247 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
248 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
249 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
250 |               }
251 |             }
252 |           }
253 | 
254 |           if (condition != null) {
255 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
256 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
257 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
258 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
259 |               // scope on false.
260 |               //
261 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
262 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
263 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
264 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
265 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
266 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
267 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
268 | 
269 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
272 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
273 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
274 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
275 |               }
276 |               newScope =
277 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
278 |                       condition,
279 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
280 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
281 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
282 |             } else {
283 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
284 |               // of the loop.
285 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
286 |                 conditionFlowScope =
287 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
288 |               }
289 |               newScope =
290 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
291 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
292 |             }
293 |           }
294 |           break;
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
298 |     }
299 |     return result;
300 |   }
