===== M0416 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

===== M0435 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineObjectLiteral(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 561-608
Documentation:

Method code:
561 |     private void defineObjectLiteral(NodeTraversal t, Node objectLit) {
562 |       // Handle the @lends annotation.
563 |       JSType type = null;
564 |       JSDocInfo info = objectLit.getJSDocInfo();
565 |       if (info != null &&
566 |           info.getLendsName() != null) {
567 |         String lendsName = info.getLendsName();
568 |         Var lendsVar = scope.getVar(lendsName);
569 |         if (lendsVar == null) {
570 |           compiler.report(
571 |               JSError.make(sourceName, objectLit, UNKNOWN_LENDS, lendsName));
572 |         } else {
573 |           type = lendsVar.getType();
574 |           if (type == null) {
575 |             type = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
576 |           }
577 |           if (!type.isSubtype(typeRegistry.getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
578 |             compiler.report(
579 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, objectLit, LENDS_ON_NON_OBJECT,
580 |                     lendsName, type.toString()));
581 |             type = null;
582 |           } else {
583 |             objectLit.setJSType(type);
584 |           }
585 |         }
586 |       }
587 | 
588 |       info = getBestJSDocInfo(objectLit);
589 |       Node lValue = getBestLValue(objectLit);
590 |       String lValueName = getBestLValueName(lValue);
591 |       boolean createdEnumType = false;
592 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
593 |         type = createEnumTypeFromNodes(objectLit, lValueName, info, lValue);
594 |         createdEnumType = true;
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       if (type == null) {
598 |         type = typeRegistry.createAnonymousObjectType();
599 |       }
600 | 
601 |       setDeferredType(objectLit, type);
602 | 
603 |       // If this is an enum, the properties were already taken care of above.
604 |       if (!createdEnumType) {
605 |         processObjectLitProperties(
606 |             t, objectLit, ObjectType.cast(objectLit.getJSType()));
607 |       }
608 |     }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.TypedScopeCreator(AbstractCompiler,CodingConvention)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 165-172
Documentation:

Method code:
165 |   TypedScopeCreator(AbstractCompiler compiler,
166 |       CodingConvention codingConvention) {
167 |     this.compiler = compiler;
168 |     this.validator = compiler.getTypeValidator();
169 |     this.codingConvention = codingConvention;

440 |     @Override
441 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
442 |         Node parent) {
443 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
444 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
446 |       }
447 | 
448 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
449 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
450 |       boolean descend = parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
451 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
452 | 
453 |       if (descend) {
454 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
455 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.

===== M0454 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 302-463

Method code:
302 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
303 |     switch (n.getType()) {
304 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
305 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
306 |         break;
307 | 
308 |       case Token.NAME:
309 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
310 |         break;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.GETPROP:
313 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
314 |         break;
315 | 
316 |       case Token.AND:
317 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
318 |             .createChildFlowScope();
319 |         break;
320 | 
321 |       case Token.OR:
322 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
323 |             .createChildFlowScope();
324 |         break;
325 | 
326 |       case Token.HOOK:
327 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
328 |         break;
329 | 
330 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
331 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.CALL:
335 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
336 |         break;
337 | 
338 |       case Token.NEW:
339 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
343 |       case Token.ADD:
344 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.POS:
348 |       case Token.NEG:
349 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
350 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
351 |         break;
352 | 
353 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
354 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
355 |         break;
356 | 
357 |       case Token.THIS:
358 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
359 |         break;
360 | 
361 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
362 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
363 |       case Token.LSH:
364 |       case Token.RSH:
365 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
366 |       case Token.URSH:
367 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
374 |       case Token.DIV:
375 |       case Token.MOD:
376 |       case Token.BITAND:
377 |       case Token.BITXOR:
378 |       case Token.BITOR:
379 |       case Token.MUL:
380 |       case Token.SUB:
381 |       case Token.DEC:
382 |       case Token.INC:
383 |       case Token.BITNOT:
384 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
385 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
386 |         break;
387 | 
388 |       case Token.LP:
389 |       case Token.GET_REF:
390 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
391 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
392 |         break;
393 | 
394 |       case Token.COMMA:
395 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
396 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
397 |         break;
398 | 
399 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
400 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
401 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
402 |         break;
403 | 
404 |       case Token.DELPROP:
405 |       case Token.LT:
406 |       case Token.LE:
407 |       case Token.GT:
408 |       case Token.GE:
409 |       case Token.NOT:
410 |       case Token.EQ:
411 |       case Token.NE:
412 |       case Token.SHEQ:
413 |       case Token.SHNE:
414 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
415 |       case Token.IN:
416 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
417 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.GETELEM:
421 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
425 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
426 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
427 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.SWITCH:
432 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.VAR:
436 |       case Token.RETURN:
437 |       case Token.THROW:
438 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.CATCH:
442 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
443 |         break;
444 |     }
445 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
446 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
447 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
448 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
449 | 
450 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
451 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
452 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
453 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
454 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
455 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
456 |         }
457 | 
458 |         n.setJSType(castType);
459 |       }
460 |     }
461 | 
462 |     return scope;
463 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 494-540
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
494 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
495 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
496 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
497 |     switch (left.getType()) {
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String varName = left.getString();
500 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
501 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
502 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
503 |         }
504 | 
505 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
506 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
507 |         // This has two purposes:
508 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
509 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
510 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
511 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
512 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
513 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
514 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
515 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
516 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
517 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
518 |         }
519 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
520 |             resultType : null);
521 | 
522 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
523 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
524 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
525 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
526 |         }
527 |         break;
528 |       case Token.GETPROP:
529 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
530 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
531 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
532 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
533 |               resultType);
534 |         }
535 | 
536 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
537 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
538 |         break;
539 |     }
540 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createEnumTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 944-990
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new enum type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - An object literal that needs an enum type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with an enum tag in the jsdoc.
     *
     * This function will always create an enum type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The node of the enum.
     * @param name The enum's name
     * @param info The {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the enum definition.
     * @param lValueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned.
     */
Method code:
944 |     private EnumType createEnumTypeFromNodes(Node rValue, String name,
945 |         JSDocInfo info, Node lValueNode) {
946 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(info);
947 |       Preconditions.checkState(info.hasEnumParameterType());
948 | 
949 |       EnumType enumType = null;
950 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName()) {
951 |         // Handle an aliased enum.
952 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
953 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof EnumType) {
954 |           enumType = (EnumType) var.getType();
955 |         }
956 |       }
957 | 
958 |       if (enumType == null) {
959 |         JSType elementsType =
960 |             info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
961 |         enumType = typeRegistry.createEnumType(name, elementsType);
962 | 
963 |         if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
964 |           // collect enum elements
965 |           Node key = rValue.getFirstChild();
966 |           while (key != null) {
967 |             String keyName = NodeUtil.getStringValue(key);
968 |             if (keyName == null) {
969 |               // GET and SET don't have a String value;
970 |               compiler.report(
971 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, key, ENUM_NOT_CONSTANT, keyName));
972 |             } else if (enumType.hasOwnProperty(keyName)) {
973 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, key, ENUM_DUP, keyName));
974 |             } else if (!codingConvention.isValidEnumKey(keyName)) {
975 |               compiler.report(
976 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, key, ENUM_NOT_CONSTANT, keyName));
977 |             } else {
978 |               enumType.defineElement(keyName, key);
979 |             }
980 |             key = key.getNext();
981 |           }
982 |         }
983 |       }
984 | 
985 |       if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
986 |         typeRegistry.declareType(name, enumType.getElementsType());
987 |       }
988 | 
989 |       return enumType;
990 |     }

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 461-813
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.LP:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.GET_REF:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.NULL:
505 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.NUMBER:
509 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
510 |         break;
511 | 
512 |       case Token.STRING:
513 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
514 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
515 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
516 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
517 |         }
518 |         break;
519 | 
520 |       case Token.GET:
521 |       case Token.SET:
522 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
526 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.REGEXP:
530 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.GETPROP:
534 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
535 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
536 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.GETELEM:
540 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
541 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
542 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
543 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
544 |         typeable = false;
545 |         break;
546 | 
547 |       case Token.VAR:
548 |         visitVar(t, n);
549 |         typeable = false;
550 |         break;
551 | 
552 |       case Token.NEW:
553 |         visitNew(t, n);
554 |         typeable = true;
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.CALL:
558 |         visitCall(t, n);
559 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
560 |         break;
561 | 
562 |       case Token.RETURN:
563 |         visitReturn(t, n);
564 |         typeable = false;
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.DEC:
568 |       case Token.INC:
569 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
570 |         validator.expectNumber(
571 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
572 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
573 |         break;
574 | 
575 |       case Token.NOT:
576 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.VOID:
580 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
581 |         break;
582 | 
583 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
584 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
585 |         break;
586 | 
587 |       case Token.BITNOT:
588 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
589 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
590 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
591 |               childType.toString());
592 |         }
593 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
594 |         break;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.POS:
597 |       case Token.NEG:
598 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
599 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
600 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EQ:
604 |       case Token.NE: {
605 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
606 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
607 | 
608 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
609 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
610 |         TernaryValue result =
611 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
612 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
613 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
614 |             result = result.not();
615 |           }
616 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
617 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
618 |         }
619 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
620 |         break;
621 |       }
622 | 
623 |       case Token.SHEQ:
624 |       case Token.SHNE: {
625 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
626 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
627 | 
628 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
629 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
630 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
631 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
632 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
633 |               rightType.toString());
634 |         }
635 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
636 |         break;
637 |       }
638 | 
639 |       case Token.LT:
640 |       case Token.LE:
641 |       case Token.GT:
642 |       case Token.GE:
643 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
644 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
645 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
646 |           validator.expectNumber(
647 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
648 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
649 |           validator.expectNumber(
650 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
651 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
652 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
653 |           // OK.
654 |         } else {
655 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
656 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
657 |           // should match a string context.
658 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
659 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
660 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
661 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
662 |           message = "right side of comparison";
663 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
664 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
665 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
666 |         }
667 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
668 |         break;
669 | 
670 |       case Token.IN:
671 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
672 |         right = n.getLastChild();
673 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
674 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
675 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
676 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
677 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
678 |         break;
679 | 
680 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
681 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
682 |         right = n.getLastChild();
683 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
684 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
685 | 
686 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
687 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
688 |         validator.expectActualObject(
689 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
690 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
691 |         break;
692 | 
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
694 |         visitAssign(t, n);
695 |         typeable = false;
696 |         break;
697 | 
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
703 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
704 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
705 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
706 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
707 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
708 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
709 |       case Token.LSH:
710 |       case Token.RSH:
711 |       case Token.URSH:
712 |       case Token.DIV:
713 |       case Token.MOD:
714 |       case Token.BITOR:
715 |       case Token.BITXOR:
716 |       case Token.BITAND:
717 |       case Token.SUB:
718 |       case Token.ADD:
719 |       case Token.MUL:
720 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
721 |         break;
722 | 
723 |       case Token.DELPROP:
724 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
725 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
726 |         }
727 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       case Token.CASE:
731 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
732 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
733 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
734 |         typeable = false;
735 |         break;
736 | 
737 |       case Token.WITH: {
738 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
739 |         childType = getJSType(child);
740 |         validator.expectObject(
741 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
742 |         typeable = false;
743 |         break;
744 |       }
745 | 
746 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
747 |         visitFunction(t, n);
748 |         break;
749 | 
750 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
751 |       case Token.LABEL:
752 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
753 |       case Token.SWITCH:
754 |       case Token.BREAK:
755 |       case Token.CATCH:
756 |       case Token.TRY:
757 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
758 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
759 |       case Token.BLOCK:
760 |       case Token.EMPTY:
761 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
762 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
763 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
764 |       case Token.THROW:
765 |         typeable = false;
766 |         break;
767 | 
768 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
769 |       case Token.DO:
770 |       case Token.FOR:
771 |       case Token.IF:
772 |       case Token.WHILE:
773 |         typeable = false;
774 |         break;
775 | 
776 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
777 |       case Token.AND:
778 |       case Token.HOOK:
779 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
780 |       case Token.OR:
781 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
782 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
783 |         } else {
784 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
785 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
786 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
787 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
788 |           } else {
789 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
790 |           }
791 |         }
792 |         if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
793 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
794 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
795 |           }
796 |         }
797 |         break;
798 | 
799 |       default:
800 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
801 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
802 |         break;
803 |     }
804 | 
805 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
806 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
807 | 
808 |     if (typeable) {
809 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
810 |     }
811 | 
812 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
813 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.createTemplateNode()

// e.g., source-name, between all nodes.

   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.

Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)

Original range: 84-106

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param msg An extra message for the mismatch warning, if necessary.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
370 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
371 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
372 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
373 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
374 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
375 |         // the line numbers of both types.
376 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
377 |       } else {
378 |         mismatch(t, n, msg, rightType, leftType);
379 |       }
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 |     return true;
383 |   }

===== M0499 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':
296 |       case '=':
297 |       case EOF_CHAR:
298 |       case '\n':
299 |         return false;
300 | 
301 |       default:

303 |     }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 169-913
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
169 |   boolean parse() {
170 |     int lineno;
171 |     int charno;
172 | 
173 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
174 |     JSTypeExpression type;
175 | 
176 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
177 |     skipEOLs();
178 | 
179 |     JsDocToken token = next();
180 | 
181 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
182 | 
183 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
184 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
185 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
186 |       token = blockInfo.token;
187 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
188 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
189 |       }
190 |     } else {
191 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
192 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
193 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
194 |         // what it was.
195 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
200 |     retry: for (;;) {
201 |       switch (token) {
202 |         case ANNOTATION:
203 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
204 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
205 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
206 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
207 | 
208 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
209 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
210 |             if (annotation == null) {
211 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
212 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
213 |             } else {
214 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
215 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
216 | 
217 |               switch (annotation) {
218 |                 case AUTHOR:
219 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
220 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
221 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
222 | 
223 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
224 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
225 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                     } else {
227 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
228 |                     }
229 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
230 |                   } else {
231 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
232 |                   }
233 |                   continue retry;
234 | 
235 |                 case CONSTANT:
236 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
237 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
238 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                   }
240 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
241 |                   continue retry;
242 | 
243 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
244 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
245 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
246 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
247 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
248 |                     } else {
249 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
250 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                     }
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case DEPRECATED:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 | 
262 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
263 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
264 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
265 | 
266 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
267 | 
268 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
269 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
270 |                   }
271 | 
272 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
273 |                   continue retry;
274 | 
275 |                 case INTERFACE:
276 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
277 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
278 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
279 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                     } else {
281 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
282 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
283 |                     }
284 |                   }
285 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
286 |                   continue retry;
287 | 
288 |                 case DESC:
289 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
290 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
291 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
292 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
293 |                     continue retry;
294 |                   } else {
295 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
296 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
297 | 
298 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
299 | 
300 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
301 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
302 |                     continue retry;
303 |                   }
304 | 
305 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
306 |                   String fileOverview = "";
307 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
308 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
309 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
310 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
311 | 
312 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
313 | 
314 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
315 |                   } else {
316 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
317 |                   }
318 | 
319 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
320 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
321 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
322 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
323 |                   }
324 |                   continue retry;
325 | 
326 |                 case LICENSE:
327 |                 case PRESERVE:
328 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
329 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
330 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
331 | 
332 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
333 | 
334 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
335 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
336 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
337 |                     }
338 |                   }
339 | 
340 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
341 |                   continue retry;
342 | 
343 |                 case ENUM:
344 |                   token = next();
345 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
346 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
347 | 
348 |                   type = null;
349 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
350 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
351 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
352 |                   }
353 | 
354 |                   if (type == null) {
355 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
356 |                   }
357 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
358 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
359 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
360 |                   }
361 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
362 |                   continue retry;
363 | 
364 |                 case EXPORT:
365 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
366 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
367 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
368 |                   }
369 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
370 |                   continue retry;
371 | 
372 |                 case EXTERNS:
373 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
374 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
375 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
376 |                   }
377 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
378 |                   continue retry;
379 | 
380 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
381 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
382 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
383 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
384 |                   }
385 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
386 |                   continue retry;
387 | 
388 |                 case EXTENDS:
389 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
390 |                   skipEOLs();
391 |                   token = next();
392 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
393 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
394 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
395 | 
396 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
397 |                     token = next();
398 |                     matchingRc = true;
399 |                   }
400 | 
401 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
402 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
403 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
404 | 
405 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
406 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
407 | 
408 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
409 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
410 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
411 |                     }
412 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
413 | 
414 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
415 |                       // record the extended type, check later
416 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
417 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
418 |                     } else {
419 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
420 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
421 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
422 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
423 |                             lineno, charno);
424 |                       }
425 |                     }
426 |                     token = next();
427 |                     if (matchingRc) {
428 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
429 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
430 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
431 |                       }
432 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
433 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
434 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
435 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
436 |                     }
437 |                   } else {
438 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
439 |                   }
440 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
441 |                   continue retry;
442 | 
443 |                 case HIDDEN:
444 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
445 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
446 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
447 |                   }
448 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
449 |                   continue retry;
450 | 
451 |                 case LENDS:
452 |                   skipEOLs();
453 | 
454 |                   matchingRc = false;
455 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
456 |                     token = next();
457 |                     matchingRc = true;
458 |                   }
459 | 
460 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
461 |                     token = next();
462 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
463 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
464 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
465 |                     }
466 |                   } else {
467 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
468 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
469 |                   }
470 | 
471 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
472 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
473 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
474 |                   }
475 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
476 |                   continue retry;
477 | 
478 |                 case MEANING:
479 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
480 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
481 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
482 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
483 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
484 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
485 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
486 |                   }
487 |                   continue retry;
488 | 
489 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
490 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
491 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
492 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                   }
494 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
495 |                   continue retry;
496 | 
497 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
498 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
499 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
500 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
501 |                   }
502 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
503 |                   continue retry;
504 | 
505 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
506 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
507 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
508 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
509 |                   }
510 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
511 |                   continue retry;
512 | 
513 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
514 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
515 |                   continue retry;
516 | 
517 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
518 |                 case OVERRIDE:
519 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
520 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
521 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
522 |                   }
523 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
524 |                   continue retry;
525 | 
526 |                 case THROWS:
527 |                   skipEOLs();
528 |                   token = next();
529 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
530 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
531 |                   type = null;
532 | 
533 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
534 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
535 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
536 | 
537 |                     if (type == null) {
538 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
539 |                       // recovering parsing
540 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
541 |                       continue retry;
542 |                     }
543 |                   }
544 | 
545 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
546 |                   token = current();
547 | 
548 |                   // Save the throw type.
549 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
550 | 
551 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
552 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
553 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
554 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
555 | 
556 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
557 | 
558 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
559 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
560 |                     }
561 | 
562 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
563 |                   } else {
564 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
565 |                   }
566 |                   continue retry;
567 | 
568 |                 case PARAM:
569 |                   skipEOLs();
570 |                   token = next();
571 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
572 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
573 |                   type = null;
574 | 
575 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
576 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
577 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
578 | 
579 |                     if (type == null) {
580 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
581 |                       // recovering parsing
582 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
583 |                       continue retry;
584 |                     }
585 |                     skipEOLs();
586 |                     token = next();
587 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
588 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
589 |                   }
590 | 
591 |                   String name = null;
592 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
593 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
594 |                     token = next();
595 |                   }
596 | 
597 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
598 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
599 |                         lineno, charno);
600 |                   } else {
601 |                     name = stream.getString();
602 | 
603 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
604 |                       token = next();
605 | 
606 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
607 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
608 |                       // system.
609 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
610 |                         token = next();
611 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
612 |                           token = next();
613 |                         }
614 |                       }
615 | 
616 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
617 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
618 |                       } else if (type != null) {
619 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
620 |                         // already.
621 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
622 |                       }
623 |                     }
624 | 
625 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
626 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
627 |                     // for handling properties of params.
628 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
629 |                       name = null;
630 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
631 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
632 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
633 |                             lineno, charno);
634 |                       } else {
635 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
636 |                             lineno, charno);
637 |                       }
638 |                     }
639 |                   }
640 | 
641 |                   if (name == null) {
642 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
643 |                     continue retry;
644 |                   }
645 | 
646 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
647 | 
648 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
649 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
650 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
651 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
652 | 
653 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
654 | 
655 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
656 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
657 |                           paramDescription);
658 |                     }
659 | 
660 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
661 |                   } else {
662 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
663 |                   }
664 |                   continue retry;
665 | 
666 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
667 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
668 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
669 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
670 |                   }
671 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
672 |                   continue retry;
673 | 
674 |                 case PRIVATE:
675 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
676 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
677 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
678 |                   }
679 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
680 |                   continue retry;
681 | 
682 |                 case PROTECTED:
683 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
684 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
685 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
686 |                   }
687 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
688 |                   continue retry;
689 | 
690 |                 case PUBLIC:
691 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
692 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
693 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
694 |                   }
695 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
696 |                   continue retry;
697 | 
698 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
699 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
700 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
701 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
702 |                   }
703 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
704 |                   continue retry;
705 | 
706 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
707 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
708 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
709 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
710 |                   }
711 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
712 |                   continue retry;
713 | 
714 |                 case MODIFIES:
715 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
720 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case SEE:
727 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
728 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
729 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
730 | 
731 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
732 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
733 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
734 |                     } else {
735 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
736 |                     }
737 | 
738 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
739 |                   } else {
740 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
741 |                   }
742 |                   continue retry;
743 | 
744 |                 case SUPPRESS:
745 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
746 |                   continue retry;
747 | 
748 |                 case TEMPLATE:
749 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
750 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
751 | 
752 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
753 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
754 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
755 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
756 |                       templateTypeName)) {
757 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
758 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
759 |                   }
760 | 
761 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
762 |                   continue retry;
763 | 
764 |                 case VERSION:
765 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
766 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
767 | 
768 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
769 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
770 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
771 |                   } else {
772 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
773 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
774 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
775 |                     }
776 |                   }
777 | 
778 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
779 |                   continue retry;
780 | 
781 |                 case DEFINE:
782 |                 case RETURN:
783 |                 case THIS:
784 |                 case TYPE:
785 |                 case TYPEDEF:
786 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
787 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
788 | 
789 |                   Node typeNode = null;
790 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
791 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
792 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
793 |                     // it as the unknown type.
794 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
795 |                   } else {
796 |                     skipEOLs();
797 |                     token = next();
798 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
799 |                   }
800 | 
801 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
802 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
803 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
804 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
805 |                     }
806 |                   }
807 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
808 | 
809 |                   if (type == null) {
810 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
811 |                     // recovering parsing
812 |                   } else {
813 |                     switch (annotation) {
814 |                       case DEFINE:
815 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
816 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
817 |                               lineno, charno);
818 |                         }
819 |                         break;
820 | 
821 |                       case RETURN:
822 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
823 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
824 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
825 |                           break;
826 |                         }
827 | 
828 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
829 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
830 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
831 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
832 | 
833 |                           String returnDescription =
834 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
835 | 
836 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
837 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
838 |                                 returnDescription);
839 |                           }
840 | 
841 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
842 |                         } else {
843 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
844 |                         }
845 |                         continue retry;
846 | 
847 |                       case THIS:
848 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
849 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
850 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
851 |                         }
852 |                         break;
853 | 
854 |                       case TYPE:
855 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
856 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
857 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
858 |                         }
859 |                         break;
860 | 
861 |                       case TYPEDEF:
862 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
863 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
864 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
865 |                         }
866 |                         break;
867 |                     }
868 |                   }
869 | 
870 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
871 |                   continue retry;
872 |               }
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 |           break;
876 | 
877 |         case EOC:
878 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
879 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
880 |           }
881 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
882 |           return true;
883 | 
884 |         case EOF:
885 |           // discard any accumulated information
886 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
887 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
888 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
889 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
890 |           return false;
891 | 
892 |         case EOL:
893 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
894 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
895 |           }
896 |           token = next();
897 |           continue retry;
898 | 
899 |         default:
900 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
901 |             token = next();
902 |             continue retry;
903 |           } else {
904 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
905 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
906 |             continue retry;
907 |           }
908 |       }
909 | 
910 |       // next token
911 |       token = next();
912 |     }
913 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N>,JoinOp<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 120-130
Documentation:
/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
120 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
121 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
122 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
123 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0533 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }
