Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
951 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
952 |     if (resolved) {
953 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
954 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
955 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
956 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
957 |       }
958 |       return resolveResult;
959 |     }
960 |     resolved = true;
961 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
962 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
963 |     return resolveResult;
964 |   }

/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1479 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1480 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1481 |         Node n = stub.node;
1482 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1483 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1484 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1485 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1486 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1487 | 
1488 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1489 |           continue;
1490 |         }
1491 | 
1492 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1493 |         // in the type registry.
1494 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1495 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1496 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1497 | 
1498 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1499 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1500 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1501 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1502 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1503 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern, n);
1504 |         } else {
1505 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1506 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1507 |         }
1508 |       }
1509 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
447 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

451 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
452 |         return true;
453 |       }

455 |     return false;
456 |   }

===== M0551 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-487
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
478 |             unknown = true;
479 |             break;
480 |           }
481 |         }
482 |       } else {
483 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
484 |       }
485 |     }
486 |     return unknown;
487 |   }

1765 |   private static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
1766 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1767 |     int parentType = parent.getType();
1768 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
1769 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
1770 |       return n.getFirstChild();
1771 |     } else if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1772 |       return parent;
1773 |     } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1774 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
1775 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1776 |       return parent;
1777 |     }
1778 |     return null;
1779 |   }

===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 470-516
Documentation:

Method code:
470 |     @Override
471 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
472 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
473 | 
474 |       switch (n.getType()) {
475 |         case Token.CALL:
476 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
477 |           break;
478 | 
479 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
480 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
481 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
482 |           }
483 | 
484 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
485 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
486 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
487 |           }
488 |           break;
489 | 
490 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
491 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
492 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
493 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
494 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
495 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
496 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
497 |           }
498 |           break;
499 | 
500 |         case Token.CATCH:
501 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
502 |           break;
503 | 
504 |         case Token.VAR:
505 |           defineVar(n, parent);
506 |           break;
507 | 
508 |         case Token.GETPROP:
509 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
510 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
511 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
512 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
513 |           }
514 |           break;
515 |       }
516 |     }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1183-1231
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1183 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1184 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1185 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1186 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1187 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1188 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1189 |               (FunctionType) rValue.getJSType(), info, lValue)) {
1190 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1191 |       } else if (info != null) {
1192 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1193 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1194 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1195 |           } else {
1196 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1197 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1198 |           }
1199 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1200 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1201 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1202 |         } else {
1203 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1204 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1205 |             JSType knownType = null;
1206 |             if (rValue != null) {
1207 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1208 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1209 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1210 |               } else if (rValue.getType() == Token.OR) {
1211 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1212 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1213 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1214 |                 // reasons.
1215 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1216 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1217 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.getType() == Token.NAME
1218 |                     && lValue.getType() == Token.NAME
1219 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1220 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1221 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1222 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1223 |                 }
1224 |               }
1225 |             }
1226 |           }
1227 |         }
1228 |       }
1229 | 
1230 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1231 |     }

===== M0588 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1162-1170
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1162 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1163 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1164 |       return -1;
1165 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1167 |     } else {
1168 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1169 |     }
1170 |   }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1745-1762
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
1745 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
1746 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1747 |     if (info == null) {
1748 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
1749 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
1750 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1751 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1752 |         if (info == null && parent.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
1753 |           info = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
1754 |         }
1755 |       } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1756 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1757 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1758 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1759 |       }
1760 |     }
1761 |     return info;
1762 |   }

     * Process an object literal and all the types on it.

     *     type, because of the lends annotation.

616 |     void processObjectLitProperties(
617 |         NodeTraversal t, Node objLit, ObjectType objLitType) {
618 |       for (Node keyNode = objLit.getFirstChild(); keyNode != null;

===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1365-1519
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1365 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1366 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1367 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1368 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1369 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1370 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1371 | 
1372 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1373 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1374 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1375 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1376 | 
1377 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1378 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1379 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1380 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1381 |         }
1382 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1383 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1385 | 
1386 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1387 |         return createOptionalType(
1388 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1389 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1390 | 
1391 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1392 |         return createOptionalType(
1393 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1394 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1395 | 
1396 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1397 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1398 | 
1399 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1400 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1401 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1402 | 
1403 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1404 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1405 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1406 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1407 |           builder.addAlternate(
1408 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1409 |         }
1410 |         return builder.build();
1411 | 
1412 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1413 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1414 | 
1415 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1416 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1417 | 
1418 |       case Token.STRING:
1419 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1420 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1421 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1422 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1423 |         }
1424 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1425 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1426 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1427 |           if (typeList != null &&
1428 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1429 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1430 |             JSType parameterType =
1431 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1432 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1433 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1434 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1435 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1436 |               JSType indexType =
1437 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1438 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1439 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1440 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1441 |             }
1442 |           }
1443 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1444 |         } else {
1445 |           return namedType;
1446 |         }
1447 | 
1448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1449 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1450 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1451 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1452 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1453 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1454 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1455 |           thisType =
1456 |               ObjectType.cast(
1457 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1458 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1459 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1460 |           if (thisType == null) {
1461 |             reporter.warning(
1462 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1463 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1464 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1465 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1466 |                 sourceName,
1467 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1468 |           }
1469 | 
1470 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1471 |           current = current.getNext();
1472 |         }
1473 | 
1474 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1475 | 
1476 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1477 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1478 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1479 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1480 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1481 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1482 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1483 |               } else {
1484 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1485 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1486 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1487 |               }
1488 |             } else {
1489 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1490 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1491 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1492 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1493 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1494 |                   reporter.warning(
1495 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1496 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1497 |                 }
1498 |               } else {
1499 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1500 |               }
1501 |             }
1502 |           }
1503 |           current = current.getNext();
1504 |         }
1505 | 
1506 |         JSType returnType =
1507 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1508 | 
1509 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1510 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1511 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1512 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1513 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1514 |             .build();
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1518 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1519 |   }

===== M0635 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-188
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
93 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
94 | 
95 |     switch (n.getType()) {
96 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
97 |       case Token.NAME:
98 |         if (parent == null) {
99 |           return;
100 |         }
101 | 
102 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
103 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
104 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
105 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
106 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
107 |           return;
108 |         }
109 | 
110 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
111 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
112 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
113 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
114 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
115 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
116 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
117 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
118 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
119 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
120 |         if (docInfo == null &&
121 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
122 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
123 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
124 |         }
125 | 
126 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
127 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
128 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
129 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
130 |         }
131 | 
132 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
133 |         // we can do.
134 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
135 |           return;
136 |         }
137 | 
138 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
139 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
140 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
141 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
142 |           return;
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.GETPROP:
149 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
150 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
151 |         //
152 |         // 1)
153 |         // /** @deprecated */
154 |         // obj.prop = ...
155 |         //
156 |         // 2)
157 |         // /** @deprecated */
158 |         // obj.prop;
159 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
160 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
161 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
162 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
163 |           if (docInfo == null) {
164 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
165 |           }
166 |           if (docInfo != null) {
167 |             ObjectType lhsType =
168 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
169 |             if (lhsType != null) {
170 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
171 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
172 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
173 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
174 |               }
175 | 
176 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
177 |               ObjectType propType =
178 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
179 |               if (propType != null) {
180 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
181 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
182 |               }
183 |             }
184 |           }
185 |         }
186 |         break;
187 |     }
188 |   }

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0661 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

1245 |       if (!used) {
1246 |         used = true;
1247 |         return this;
1248 |       } else {

===== M0663 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

===== M0669 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1640-1679
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1640 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1641 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1642 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1643 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1644 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1645 |       // {?} - right curly
1646 |       // {?=} - equals
1647 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1648 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1649 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1650 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1651 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1652 |       token = next();
1653 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1654 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1655 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1656 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1657 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1658 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1659 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1660 |       }
1661 | 
1662 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1663 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1664 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1665 |     } else {
1666 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1667 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1668 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1669 |           next();
1670 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1671 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1672 |           next();
1673 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1674 |         }
1675 |       }
1676 | 
1677 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1678 |     }
1679 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
340 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
341 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
342 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
343 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
344 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
345 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
346 |         // the line numbers of both types.
347 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
348 |       } else {
349 |         mismatch(t, n,
350 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
351 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
352 |             rightType, leftType);
353 |       }
354 |       return false;
355 |     }
356 |     return true;
357 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 178-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
178 |   @Override
179 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
180 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
181 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
182 |     // show up in the type registry.
183 |     Scope newScope = null;
184 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
185 |     if (parent == null) {
186 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
187 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
188 | 
189 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
190 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
191 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
192 |     } else {
193 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
194 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
195 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
196 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
201 | 
202 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
203 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
204 |     // build properties on.
205 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
206 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
207 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
208 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
209 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
210 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
211 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
212 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
213 |         }
214 |       }
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     if (parent == null) {
218 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
219 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
220 |     }
221 |     return newScope;
222 |   }

===== M0686 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 436-488
Documentation:

Method code:
436 |   @Override
437 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
438 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
439 |       return true;
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     // Union types
443 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
444 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
445 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
446 |       return false;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // record types
450 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
451 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // Interfaces
455 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
456 |     // to the interface instance.
457 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
458 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
459 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
460 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
461 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
462 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
463 |           return true;
464 |         }
465 |       }
466 |     }
467 | 
468 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
469 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
470 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
471 |           return true;
472 |         }
473 |       }
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     // other prototype based objects
477 |     if (that != null) {
478 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
479 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
480 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
481 |         // to avoid guessing.
482 |         return true;
483 |       }
484 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     return false;
488 |   }
